forceful."],"synonyms":[{"word":"pointed"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"He gave me an emphatic no when I asked him out.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Stated with conviction."]},{"glosses":["Belonging to a set of English tense forms comprising the auxiliary verb do plus an infinitive without to."],"topics":["grammar","human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"In Irish, the emphatic form of \"mé\" is \"mise\".","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Relating to an emphatic pronoun, or a noun with an emphatic suffix attached to it."],"topics":["grammar","human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]},{"antonyms":[{"word":"plain"}],"glosses":["Belonging to a series of obstruent consonants in several Afro-Asiatic languages that are distinguished by a guttural (co-)articulation.","Phrayngealized consonants in Arabic, Hebrew, other Northwest Semitic languages, and Berber languages."],"topics":["human-sciences","linguistics","phonology","sciences"]},{"antonyms":[{"word":"plain"}],"glosses":["Belonging to a series of obstruent consonants in several Afro-Asiatic languages that are distinguished by a guttural (co-)articulation.","Ejective consonants in Ge'ez, Amharic, other Ethiopic Semitic languages, Chadic and Cushitic languages."],"topics":["human-sciences","linguistics","phonology","sciences"]},{"glosses":["Referring to the above consonants as well as /ħ/ and /ʕ/ (these being seen as emphatic equivalents of /h/ and /ʔ/)."],"qualifier":"archaic except in layman’s use","topics":["human-sciences","linguistics","phonology","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɛmˈfætɪk/"},{"ipa":"/ɪm-/"},{"ipa":"/əmˈfætək/"}],"synonyms":[{"word":"distinct"}],"upstream_line":354811,"upstream_offset":1710118560,"upstream_record_sha256":"67e3b1a4353feff4ffa8cdadaffe554771bfbb3a85bd5af624872a914650612f","word":"emphatic"}
